from 15:00 inclusive to next day 16:00 exclusive\n    total=0\n    cur=d.replace(hour=15)\n    stop=(d+timedelta(days=1)).replace(hour=16)\n    h=cur\n    while h < stop:\n        total += by_hour[h.strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H\")]\n        h += timedelta(hours=1)\n    dow=d.strftime(\"%A\")\n    print(d.strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d\"), dow, total)\n    starts.append((dow, total))\n    d += timedelta(days=1)\n\n# specifically Thursday (yesterday's pattern day) and Fridays\nprint(\"\\nBy weekday mean of those 25h slices:\")\nagg=defaultdict(list)\nfor dow,total in starts:\n    agg[dow].append(total)\nfor dow,vals in agg.items():\n    print(dow, vals, \"mean\", sum(vals)/len(vals), \"min\", min(vals), \"max\", max(vals))\n\n# Also: how many of the 62 in current window happened in first 23h vs \n# Distribution of posts in the UPCOMING hours based on same weekday last weeks\n# Upcoming: Fri 15:00 UTC to Sat 16:00 UTC\nprint(\"\\nOnly Friday-start 25h slices (Fri 15:00 UTC to Sat 16:00 UTC):\")\nfor dow,total in starts:\n    pass\n# reprint with dates already has Friday\n\n# Probability final in 65-89 means additional posts in [3, 27] since current is 62\n# but current includes up to 14:33 and partial 15:00 hour is 0 so far. \n# The historical slice starts at 15:00 so additional ~= that slice. Need 3 to 27 inclusive.\nprint(\"\\nIf additional ~ historical slice, P(add in 3..27) i.e. final 65-89\")\nvals=[t for _,t in starts]\nn=len(vals)\ninband=sum(1 for t in vals if 3<=t<=27)\nunder=sum(1 for t in vals if t<3)  # final <=64\nover=sum(1 for t in vals if t>27)  # final >=90\nprint(\"n\", n, \"in 65-89\", inband, \"under65\", under, \"over89\", over)\nprint(\"p_yes\", inband/n if n else None)\n\n# tighter: only weekdays Fri or all weekdays\nfri=[t for dow,t in starts if dow==\"Friday\"]\nprint(\"friday slices\", fri, \"p in band\", sum(1 for t in fri if 3<=t<=27)/len(fri) if fri else None)\n\n# Also look at pace since the burst ended.
